cosmo_artist_affair = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			}
		}
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_prestige = 5
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_adm_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_adm_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asort_events.115
					}				
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_I
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			} 
		}			
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
		has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					adm_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_scientist_affair_adm					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					adm_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_scientist_affair_adm
				duration = 10950  
				desc = cosmo_scientist_affair_adm_DESC
			}					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
			}
			FROM = {
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II	
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_prestige = 5
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_adm_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_adm_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asortpu_events.115
					}				
			}			
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 5
				mutual = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					liberty_desire = 25
				}
				add_liberty_desire = -5			
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_artist_II
				duration = 5475  
			}
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 5
				mutual = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					liberty_desire = 25
				}
				add_liberty_desire = -5			
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			FROM = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_artist_III			
				}
			}	
			}
			FROM = {
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
				add_country_modifier = { 
					name = cosmo_affair_artist_I
					duration = 5475  
				} 
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 5
				mutual = yes
			}				
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor			
	}
	custom_tooltip = cosmo_degeneracy_100
	hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
			which = cosmoDegeneracyVariable
			value = 100
			}			
	}		
}

					liberty_desire = 25
				}
				add_liberty_desire = -5			
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_scientist_I
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			FROM = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_scientist_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_scientist_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_scientist_III			
				}
			}	
			}
			FROM = {
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
				add_country_modifier = { 
					name = cosmo_affair_scientist_I
					duration = 5475  
				} 
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 5
				mutual = yes
			}				
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor			
	}	
	custom_tooltip = cosmo_degeneracy_100
	hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
			which = cosmoDegeneracyVariable
			value = 100
			}			
	}	
}

cosmo_philosopher_affair = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			}
		}
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_adm_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_adm_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asort_events.115
					}				
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_philosopher_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_philosopher_I
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			} 
		}			
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
		has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					adm_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_scientist_affair_adm					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					adm_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_scientist_affair_adm
				duration = 10950  
				desc = cosmo_scientist_affair_adm_DESC
			}					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III			
				}
			}
			FROM = {
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			}
		}		
		if = {

				FROM = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_II	
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_affair_philosopher_III			
				}
				}

			FROM = {

				name = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			} 
		}			
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
		}
		FROM = {
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					monthly_income = 100
				}
				add_years_of_income = 0.025 
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						monthly_income = 100
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = 0.05 
			}
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_treasurer_affair_I
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			} 
		}						
		}
		FROM = {
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 2
				mutual = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					liberty_desire = 25
				}
				add_liberty_desire = -5			
			}		
		}		
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor			
	}
	custom_tooltip = cosmo_degeneracy_100
	hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
			which = cosmoDegeneracyVariable
			value = 100
			}			
	}		
}

cosmo_statesman_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			}
		}
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			change_government_reform_progress = 25			
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_dip_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_dip_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asort_events.115
					}				
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_I
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			} 
		}			
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
		has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					dip_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_scientist_affair_dip					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					dip_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_scientist_affair_dip
				duration = 10950  
				desc = cosmo_scientist_affair_dip_DESC
			}					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
		}
		FROM = {
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			change_government_reform_progress = 25			
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			if = {

					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_dip_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_dip_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asortpu_events.115
					}				
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_III			
				}
			}

				name = cosmo_statesman_affair_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_statesman_affair_I
		}

			}
		}
		if = {

			change_government_reform_progress = 25			
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_trader_affair_III
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_trader_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

			NOT = {

				has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_trader_affair_III			
				}
			}

				name = cosmo_consort_trader_affair_II
				duration = 5475  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_trader_affair_I

			}

				mutual = yes
			}

					liberty_desire = 25
				}

			}

			}

cosmo_consort_commandant_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

					has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_commandant_affair_1

					has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_commandant_affair_3
					has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_commandant_affair_4
				}
			}

		}

		cosmo_advisor_1 = {

				name = cosmo_consort_commandant_affair_1

				name = cosmo_consort_commandant_affair_2

				desc = cosmo_quartermaster_affair_I_DESC

				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_theologian_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_estate_loyalty = {
				estate = estate_church
				loyalty = 5
				}
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_theologian_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_adm_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_adm_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asortpu_events.115
					}				
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_theologian_affair_II
				duration = 9125  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_theologian_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_theologian_affair_I
				duration = 9125  
				
	
			} 
		}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 2
				mutual = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					liberty_desire = 25
				}
				add_liberty_desire = -5			
			}		
		}		
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor			
	}
	custom_tooltip = cosmo_degeneracy_100
	hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
			which = cosmoDegeneracyVariable
			value = 100
			}			
	}		
}

					id = cosmo_asort_events.115
					}				
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
				duration = 1825  
				desc = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt			
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
				duration = 1825  
				desc = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I_DESC
	
			} 
		}				
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
		has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					mil_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_scientist_affair_mil					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					mil_tech = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_scientist_affair_mil
				duration = 10950  
				desc = cosmo_scientist_affair_mil_DESC
			}					
		}
	}	
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
		}
		FROM = {
		if = {#scripted
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III			
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_army_professionalism = 0.05
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III
				duration = 9125  
	
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_mil_continue
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = cosmo_mil_affair_full
				country_event = {#end event
					id = cosmo_asortpu_events.115
					}				
			}			
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I			
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_mostly_adopted_reforms_tt
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
				duration = 9125  
				
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_II
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_III			
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_explored_reforms_tt			
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_recruitmaster_affair_I
				duration = 9125  
				
	
			} 
		}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_trust = {
				who = ROOT
				value = 2
				mutual = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					liberty_desire = 25
				}
				add_liberty_desire = -5			
			}		
		}		
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor			
	}
	custom_tooltip = cosmo_degeneracy_100
	hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
			which = cosmoDegeneracyVariable
			value = 100
			}			
	}		
}

cosmo_consort_grand_captain_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
					}
				}
			}
			add_army_professionalism = 0.05
		}		
		trigger_switch = {
		on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
		cosmo_advisor_1 = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
				duration = 9125  
				desc = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3				
			}
		cosmo_advisor_2 = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
				duration = 9125  
				desc = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3				
			}
		cosmo_advisor_3 = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3
				duration = 9125  
				desc = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4				
			}
		cosmo_advisor_4 = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
				duration = 9125  
				desc = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3							
			}			
		}
		hidden_effect = {
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_advisor_1
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_advisor_2
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_advisor_3
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_advisor_4		
		}				
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_subjects_advisor

		}
		FROM = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3
						has_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
					}
				}
			}
			add_army_professionalism = 0.05
		}						
		}		
		trigger_switch = {
		on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
		cosmo_advisor_1 = {
			FROM = {
			add_country_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
				duration = 9125  
				desc = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3	
			}
			}
		cosmo_advisor_2 = {

				duration = 9125  
				desc = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_2_DESC
	
			}
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_4
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_1
			remove_country_modifier = cosmo_consort_grand_captain_affair_3	
			}
